<div class="kicker">BOEK 2 · HOOFDSTUK 1 · ECONOMIE · 4 VWO</div>
<div class="kicker">2.1 KOSTEN EN OPBRENGSTEN</div>
<div class="cover-title">Wat houd je<br>eraan over?</div>
<div class="lead">Veel verkopen is niet hetzelfde als veel verdienen. In dit hoofdstuk ontdek je waar het geld van een onderneming blijft.</div>

Een klas verkoopt lunchboxen op een festival. De kassa loopt vol. Toch is er nog geen reden om te juichen: de kraam, ingrediënten en verpakkingen moeten ook worden betaald. En wat gebeurt er als de klas honderd extra lunchboxen maakt?

Je leert dezelfde onderneming op drie manieren bekijken: **alles bij elkaar**, **per product** en **per extra product**. Woorden, berekeningen, tabellen en grafieken helpen je daarbij.

<div class="contents">
<a href="#s211"><b>2.1.1 Kostenstructuren <span style="float:right;font-size:11pt;color:#183247">2</span></b><span>Constante, variabele, totale en gemiddelde kosten</span></a>
<a href="#s212"><b>2.1.2 Opbrengsten, winst en break-even <span style="float:right;font-size:11pt;color:#183247">10</span></b><span>Van omzet naar resultaat; rekenen en tekenen</span></a>
<a href="#s213"><b>2.1.3 Marginale kosten en marginale opbrengsten <span style="float:right;font-size:11pt;color:#183247">19</span></b><span>Wat kosten en leveren extra producten op?</span></a>
<a href="#s214"><b>2.1.4 Gemengde opgaven <span style="float:right;font-size:11pt;color:#183247">30</span></b><span>Zelf gegevens kiezen, combineren en conclusies onderbouwen</span></a>
</div>

<div class="box goals"><b>Na dit hoofdstuk kun je</b><br>
kosten indelen en kostenfuncties opstellen; totale en gemiddelde kosten, opbrengsten en winst berekenen; break-even bepalen en in een grafiek herkennen; extra kosten en opbrengsten per product berekenen; en een uitspraak controleren met gegevens.</div>

<div class="box"><b>Zo werk je met dit boek</b><br>
Lees de uitleg en het uitgewerkte voorbeeld. Begeleide inoefening is voor de meeste leerlingen een normaal onderdeel van leren. Volg na de startopgaven de begeleide en zelfstandige oefening naar de doeloefening. Heb je minder tussenstappen nodig en wil je extra uitdaging? Volg de uitdagende route met de bonus. Herhaling is extra bij beide routes.<br><br>
Werk in je schrift. Neem tabellen over als de invulruimte niet genoeg is. Gebruik een rekenmachine, liniaal en ruitjespapier. Bewaar tussenuitkomsten; rond geldbedragen aan het einde af op centen.</div>

<div class="small muted">Alle ondernemingen en gegevens in de opgaven zijn lesvoorbeelden. Er wordt geen btw berekend. Bij opbrengstenopgaven wordt de genoemde productie ook verkocht, tenzij anders vermeld.</div>

<div class="page-break"></div>

<a id="s211"></a>
<div class="kicker">2.1.1 · KOSTENSTRUCTUREN</div>
# Twee keer zoveel produceren, twee keer zoveel kosten?

Fles & Co bedrukt drinkflessen. De werkplaats kost € 300 huur per maand. Het materiaal kost € 2 per fles. Deze maand wil het bedrijf niet 200, maar 400 flessen maken. Verdubbelen de kosten dan ook?

<div class="box goals"><b>Lesdoelen</b><br>
Je kunt kosten indelen als constant of variabel en je keuze uitleggen. Je kunt TCK, TVK en TK als functie van Q opschrijven. Je kunt gemiddelde kosten berekenen met de juiste eenheid. Je kunt uitleggen hoe totale en gemiddelde kosten veranderen bij meer productie.</div>

### Kijk naar wat er met het totale bedrag gebeurt

<div class="definition"><b>Constante kosten</b> veranderen niet als de productie verandert, zolang de productiecapaciteit gelijk blijft. We bekijken steeds één afgesproken periode, bijvoorbeeld een maand.</div>

<div class="definition"><b>Productiecapaciteit</b> is de maximale hoeveelheid die een onderneming in een bepaalde periode kan produceren met de beschikbare mensen en middelen.</div>

Fles & Co kan maximaal **600 flessen per maand** maken. Tot en met die capaciteit blijft de maandhuur € 300. Ook als het bedrijf een maand niets produceert, moet het de huur betalen.

<div class="definition"><b>Variabele kosten</b> veranderen wél met de hoeveelheid die een onderneming produceert. Denk aan materiaal dat voor ieder product nodig is.</div>

<figure><img src="_assets/2.1.1_fig_1.png" alt="Bij 200 en 400 flessen blijft de huur 300 euro; de materiaalkosten nemen toe van 400 naar 800 euro."><figcaption>Figuur 1. De huur blijft gelijk; het totale materiaalbedrag groeit mee.</figcaption></figure>

<div class="box warning"><b>Een maandelijkse rekening is niet automatisch constant.</b> Een energierekening kan bestaan uit een vast abonnement én stroomverbruik dat met de productie verandert. Beoordeel de onderdelen afzonderlijk.</div>

<div class="page-break"></div>

<h1 class="native-text" data-layout-height="24.6" data-layout-width="286.158" style="left:54pt;top:49.4601pt;line-height:21.6pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:18pt;font-weight:900;color:#183247">2.1.1 · Totale kosten: alles bij elkaar</span></h1>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.9994" data-layout-width="252.9152" style="left:54pt;top:90.6703pt;line-height:14.9994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.4995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Wat kosten alle flessen samen in één maand?</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.9994" data-layout-width="493.0608" style="left:54pt;top:110.2302pt;line-height:14.56pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">Fles &amp; Co betaalt </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">€ 300 huur per maand</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> en </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">€ 2 materiaal per fles</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">. Dit zijn alle kosten. </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Q</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> is het aantal</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">geproduceerde flessen per maand. Het bedrijf kan maximaal </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">600 flessen per maand</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> maken.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.3198" data-layout-width="150.4727" style="left:65.4pt;top:150.5126pt;line-height:13.3198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Totale constante kosten (TCK)</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.7595" data-layout-width="61.1769" style="left:348.4438pt;top:157.6064pt;line-height:14.7595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.2996pt;font-weight:700;color:#166b91">TCK = 300</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.4198" data-layout-width="197.629" style="left:65.4pt;top:166.6584pt;line-height:12.4198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3499pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">De huur blijft in totaal € 300, ook bij Q = 0.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.3198" data-layout-width="146.9984" style="left:65.4pt;top:191.9046pt;line-height:13.3198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Totale variabele kosten (TVK)</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.7595" data-layout-width="100.8307" style="left:348.4438pt;top:198.9983pt;line-height:14.7595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.2996pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d">TVK = 2 × Q = 2Q</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.4198" data-layout-width="191.2431" style="left:65.4pt;top:208.0504pt;line-height:12.4198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3499pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">€ 2 materiaal per fles × het aantal flessen.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.7595" data-layout-width="92.3193" style="left:348.4438pt;top:234.1893pt;line-height:14.7595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.2996pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416">TK = TCK + TVK</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.3198" data-layout-width="91.9764" style="left:65.4pt;top:236.5906pt;line-height:13.3198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Totale kosten (TK)</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.4198" data-layout-width="183.9362" style="left:65.4pt;top:252.7364pt;line-height:12.4198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3499pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">Tel de constante en variabele kosten op.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.7595" data-layout-width="83.5129" style="left:348.4438pt;top:253.1793pt;line-height:14.7595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.2996pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416">TK = 300 + 2Q</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.4796" data-layout-width="297.1699" style="left:54pt;top:281.0742pt;line-height:12.4796pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">T = totaal.</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> TCK, TVK en TK zijn hier bedragen in </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">euro per maand</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.9994" data-layout-width="207.467" style="left:54pt;top:302.0742pt;line-height:14.9994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.4995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Voorbeeld: 200 flessen in één maand</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="31.2234" data-layout-width="336.812" style="left:54pt;top:321.7462pt;line-height:14.784pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">Eerst het materiaal: TVK = 2 × 200 = € 400. Tel daarna de huur erbij:</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">TK = 300 + 400 = € 700 per maand.</span></p>

<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.9994" data-layout-width="113.4832" style="left:54pt;top:470.7423pt;line-height:14.9994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.4995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Zo lees je de grafiek</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.9994" data-layout-width="472.1296" style="left:54pt;top:490.3022pt;line-height:14.56pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">TCK</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> is horizontaal. </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">TVK</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> begint bij 0; </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">TK</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> begint bij 300. TVK en TK stijgen even snel: voor iedere</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">extra fles komt er € 2 materiaal bij.</span></p>
<figure class="native-figure" style="left:54pt;top:526pt;width:491.2756pt;height:175.1927pt"><img alt="Figuur 2. Bij elke Q ligt TK precies € 300 boven TVK: de verticale afstand is TCK." src="_assets/theory-20260921/page-005.svg"/><figcaption class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1595" data-layout-width="329.1822" style="left:0pt;top:178.1927pt;line-height:11.1595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.2996pt;font-weight:400;color:#536777">Figuur 2. Bij elke Q ligt TK precies € 300 boven TVK: de verticale afstand is TCK.</span></figcaption></figure>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.4394" data-layout-width="225.4214" style="left:66.5pt;top:730.1449pt;line-height:13.4394pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Meer productie: niet alle kosten groeien mee</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.9993" data-layout-width="458.8979" style="left:66.5pt;top:747.7049pt;line-height:14.5599pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">Van 200 naar 400 flessen </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">verdubbelt TVK</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">, maar TCK blijft € 300. Daarom stijgt TK van € 700</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">naar € 1.100: </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">de totale kosten verdubbelen niet</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">.</span></p>
</article>

<h1 class="native-text" data-layout-height="24.6" data-layout-width="315.1201" style="left:54pt;top:49.4601pt;line-height:21.6pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:18pt;font-weight:900;color:#183247">2.1.1 · Gemiddelde kosten: per product</span></h1>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.9994" data-layout-width="244.5031" style="left:54pt;top:90.6703pt;line-height:14.9994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.4995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Gemiddeld betekent: deel het totaal door Q</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.9994" data-layout-width="471.7313" style="left:54pt;top:110.2302pt;line-height:14.56pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">Bij Fles &amp; Co: € 300 huur per maand en € 2 materiaal per fles. Deel de betreffende totale kosten</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">door het aantal flessen van die maand (</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Q</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">). Zo krijg je de </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">kosten per fles</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">.</span></p>
<span aria-label="TCK gedeeld door Q" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:339.104pt;top:149.9355pt;width:27.9207pt;height:31.2493pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="24.9211" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#166b91">TCK</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="12.2107" style="left:9.3549pt;top:17.57pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#166b91">Q</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:15.2245pt;border-top:0.8pt solid #166b91"></span></span>
<span aria-label="300 gedeeld door Q" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:383.8164pt;top:149.9355pt;width:25.8347pt;height:31.2493pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="22.835" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#166b91">300</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="12.2107" style="left:8.3119pt;top:17.57pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#166b91">Q</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:15.2245pt;border-top:0.8pt solid #166b91"></span></span>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.9595" data-layout-width="176.6245" style="left:65.4pt;top:151.358pt;line-height:12.9595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.7996pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Gemiddelde constante kosten (GCK)</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="40.668" style="left:299.4363pt;top:156.794pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#166b91">GCK = </span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="15.792" style="left:369.0247pt;top:156.794pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#166b91"> = </span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.2396" data-layout-width="118.2261" style="left:65.4pt;top:167.1503pt;line-height:12.2396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">Het aandeel huur per fles.</span></p>
<span aria-label="TVK gedeeld door Q" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:339.3259pt;top:192.4755pt;width:28.5703pt;height:31.2493pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="25.5709" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d">TVK</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="12.2107" style="left:9.6797pt;top:17.57pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d">Q</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:15.2245pt;border-top:0.8pt solid #22776d"></span></span>
<span aria-label="2Q gedeeld door Q" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:384.688pt;top:192.4755pt;width:21.8225pt;height:31.2493pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="18.8224" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d">2Q</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="12.2107" style="left:6.3058pt;top:17.57pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d">Q</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:15.2245pt;border-top:0.8pt solid #22776d"></span></span>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.9595" data-layout-width="172.8231" style="left:65.4pt;top:193.898pt;line-height:12.9595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.7996pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Gemiddelde variabele kosten (GVK)</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="40.896" style="left:299.4363pt;top:199.334pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d">GVK = </span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="15.792" style="left:369.8962pt;top:199.334pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d"> = </span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="22.752" style="left:408.5105pt;top:199.334pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#22776d"> = 2</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.2396" data-layout-width="132.2609" style="left:65.4pt;top:209.6903pt;line-height:12.2396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">Het materiaalbedrag per fles.</span></p>
<span aria-label="TK gedeeld door Q" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:338.3474pt;top:235.0155pt;width:20.6536pt;height:31.2493pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="17.6597" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416">TK</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="12.2107" style="left:5.7213pt;top:17.57pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416">Q</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:15.2245pt;border-top:0.8pt solid #9c5416"></span></span>
<span aria-label="300 + 2Q gedeeld door Q" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:375.7927pt;top:235.0155pt;width:53.8088pt;height:31.2493pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="50.8092" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416">300 + 2Q</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="16.6793" data-layout-width="12.2107" style="left:22.299pt;top:17.57pt;line-height:13.6793pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.3994pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416">Q</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:15.2245pt;border-top:0.8pt solid #9c5416"></span></span>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.9595" data-layout-width="156.5805" style="left:65.4pt;top:236.438pt;line-height:12.9595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.7996pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Gemiddelde totale kosten (GTK)</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="39.924" style="left:299.4363pt;top:241.874pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416">GTK = </span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="15.792" style="left:361.001pt;top:241.874pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#9c5416"> = </span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.2397" data-layout-width="128.8134" style="left:65.4pt;top:252.2303pt;line-height:12.2396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">De volledige kosten per fles.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.4394" data-layout-width="190.8934" style="left:63pt;top:282.4102pt;line-height:13.4394pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Dus: GTK = GCK + GVK = 300 / Q + 2</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.4796" data-layout-width="251.7254" style="left:54pt;top:305.3702pt;line-height:12.4796pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">G = gemiddeld.</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> Gebruik </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Q &gt; 0</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">: delen door nul kan niet.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.9994" data-layout-width="283.0266" style="left:54pt;top:326.3703pt;line-height:14.9994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.4995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Voorbeeld: dezelfde huur, tweemaal zoveel flessen</span></h2>

</td>
<td style="left:93.3424pt;top:54.99pt;width:132.6444pt;height:24.62pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1192" data-layout-width="78.8485" style="left:51.7948pt;top:6.2505pt;line-height:12.1192pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">300 / 400 = </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">0,75</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:225.9868pt;top:54.99pt;width:132.6444pt;height:24.62pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1192" data-layout-width="78.8485" style="left:51.7948pt;top:6.2505pt;line-height:12.1192pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">800 / 400 = </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">2,00</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:358.6312pt;top:54.99pt;width:132.6444pt;height:24.62pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1192" data-layout-width="87.0897" style="left:43.5536pt;top:6.2505pt;line-height:12.1192pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">1.100 / 400 = </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">2,75</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.9994" data-layout-width="218.8666" style="left:54pt;top:435.2302pt;line-height:14.9994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.4995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Waarom daalt GTK bij meer productie?</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.9994" data-layout-width="472.0472" style="left:54pt;top:454.7902pt;line-height:14.56pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">De huur blijft in totaal € 300, maar wordt over meer flessen verdeeld. Daarom </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">halveert GCK</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> van</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">€ 1,50 naar € 0,75. GVK blijft € 2. GTK daalt dus van € 3,50 naar € 2,75, </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">maar halveert niet</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">.</span></p>
<figure class="native-figure" style="left:54pt;top:490pt;width:491.2756pt;height:175.6806pt"><img alt="Figuur 3. De afstand tussen GTK en GVK is GCK: het aandeel huur per fles wordt kleiner. Bij Q = 0 bestaan geen gemiddelde kosten." src="_assets/theory-20260921/page-006.svg"/><figcaption class="native-text" data-layout-height="25.7845" data-layout-width="458.381" style="left:0pt;top:178.6806pt;line-height:11.625pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.2996pt;font-weight:400;color:#536777">Figuur 3. De afstand tussen GTK en GVK is GCK: het aandeel huur per fles wordt kleiner. Bij Q = 0 bestaan geen</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.2996pt;font-weight:400;color:#536777">gemiddelde kosten.</span></figcaption></figure>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.9994" data-layout-width="446.0172" style="left:66.5pt;top:706.2579pt;line-height:14.56pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">Totaal is niet hetzelfde als per product.</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> Bij meer flessen stijgt hier TK, terwijl GTK daalt. Bij</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247">‘constante kosten’ blijft </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">het totaal</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.1995pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> gelijk, niet GCK.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.7195" data-layout-width="362.0005" style="left:66.5pt;top:754.3478pt;line-height:12.7195pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#183247">GVK is niet altijd constant.</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#183247"> Hier wel, omdat iedere fles € 2 aan materiaal kost.</span></p>
</article>

<div class="page-break"></div>

<h1 class="native-text" data-layout-height="23.8032" data-layout-width="348.0832" style="left:54pt;top:51.2288pt;line-height:21.12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:17.6pt;font-weight:800;color:#183e51">2.1.1 · Van kostenposten naar een conclusie</span></h1>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="21.912" data-layout-width="159.72" style="left:54pt;top:91.208pt;line-height:19.2pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:16pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Uitgewerkt voorbeeld</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="43.5292" data-layout-width="494.3709" style="left:54pt;top:118.1378pt;line-height:14pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.6pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">PlakLab maakt stickers. Het betaalt € 220 huur en € 80 abonnementskosten per maand. Folie kost € 0,80</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.6pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">en inkt € 0,20 per sticker. De capaciteit is 600 stickers per maand. Vergelijk een productie van 150 en 300</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.6pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">stickers in dezelfde maand.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.5386" data-layout-width="236.5279" style="left:54pt;top:180.1599pt;line-height:14.76pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.3pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Stap 1 Deel de kosten in en leg uit waarom</span></h2>
<table aria-label="Tabel bij Van kostenposten naar een conclusie" class="native-table" style="left:54pt;top:203pt;width:491.2756pt;height:66.6pt"><tbody>
<tr>
<th scope="col" style="left:0pt;top:0pt;width:147pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.5836" data-layout-width="53.2348" style="left:6pt;top:5.1274pt;line-height:11.76pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.8pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Kostenpost</span></p>
</th>
<th scope="col" style="left:147pt;top:0pt;width:87pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.5836" data-layout-width="58.9286" style="left:6pt;top:5.1274pt;line-height:11.76pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.8pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Soort kosten</span></p>
</th>
<th scope="col" style="left:234pt;top:0pt;width:257.2756pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.5836" data-layout-width="30.8614" style="left:6pt;top:5.1274pt;line-height:11.76pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.8pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Reden</span></p>
</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:22.2pt;width:147pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.82" data-layout-width="107.64" style="left:6pt;top:5.13pt;line-height:12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Huur en abonnementen</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:147pt;top:22.2pt;width:87pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.82" data-layout-width="42.99" style="left:6pt;top:5.13pt;line-height:12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Constant</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:234pt;top:22.2pt;width:257.2756pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.82" data-layout-width="159.79" style="left:6pt;top:5.13pt;line-height:12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Het totale maandbedrag blijft gelijk.</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:44.4pt;width:147pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.82" data-layout-width="57" style="left:6pt;top:5.13pt;line-height:12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Folie en inkt</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:147pt;top:44.4pt;width:87pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.82" data-layout-width="38.99" style="left:6pt;top:5.13pt;line-height:12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Variabel</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:234pt;top:44.4pt;width:257.2756pt;height:22.2pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.82" data-layout-width="212.74" style="left:6pt;top:5.13pt;line-height:12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Het totale bedrag groeit met het aantal stickers.</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.5386" data-layout-width="142.113" style="left:54pt;top:292.1599pt;line-height:14.76pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.3pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Stap 2 Stel de functies op</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.2928" data-layout-width="458.5722" style="left:54pt;top:313.1352pt;line-height:12.48pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.4pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Per sticker zijn de variabele kosten € 0,80 + € 0,20 = € 1. De totale bedragen zijn in euro per maand.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.8294" data-layout-width="117.4962" style="left:66pt;top:355.1521pt;line-height:14.04pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.7pt;font-weight:700;color:#007da2">TCK = 220 + 80 = 300</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.8294" data-layout-width="89.2875" style="left:66pt;top:376.1521pt;line-height:14.04pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.7pt;font-weight:700;color:#007da2">TVK = 1 × Q = Q</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.8294" data-layout-width="143.7159" style="left:66pt;top:397.1521pt;line-height:14.04pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.7pt;font-weight:700;color:#007da2">TK = TCK + TVK = 300 + Q</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.5386" data-layout-width="244.9534" style="left:54pt;top:432.1599pt;line-height:14.76pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.3pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Stap 3 Bereken totale en gemiddelde kosten</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1746" data-layout-width="347.8028" style="left:54pt;top:453.1339pt;line-height:12.36pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.3pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Vul eerst Q in. Deel daarna ieder totaalbedrag door datzelfde aantal stickers.</span></p>
<table aria-label="Tabel bij Van kostenposten naar een conclusie" class="native-table" style="left:54pt;top:475pt;width:491.2756pt;height:76.848pt"><tbody>
<tr>
<th scope="col" style="left:0pt;top:0pt;width:105pt;height:33.424pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="10.5952" style="left:6pt;top:5.1222pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Q</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="67.6908" style="left:6pt;top:16.8341pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">stickers/maand</span></p>
</th>
<th scope="col" style="left:105pt;top:0pt;width:82pt;height:33.424pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="21.3112" style="left:6pt;top:5.1222pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">TCK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="40.9008" style="left:6pt;top:16.8341pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">€/maand</span></p>
</th>
<th scope="col" style="left:187pt;top:0pt;width:140pt;height:33.424pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="21.6214" style="left:6pt;top:5.1222pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">TVK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="40.9008" style="left:6pt;top:16.8341pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">€/maand</span></p>
</th>
<th scope="col" style="left:327pt;top:0pt;width:164.2756pt;height:33.424pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="15.0884" style="left:6pt;top:5.1222pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">TK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1108" data-layout-width="40.9008" style="left:6pt;top:16.8341pt;line-height:11.28pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">€/maand</span></p>
</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:33.424pt;width:105pt;height:21.712pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.3472" data-layout-width="19.704" style="left:6pt;top:5.1248pt;line-height:11.52pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.6pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">150</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:105pt;top:33.424pt;width:82pt;height:21.712pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.3472" data-layout-width="19.704" style="left:6pt;top:5.1248pt;line-height:11.52pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.6pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">300</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:187pt;top:33.424pt;width:140pt;height:21.712pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.3472" data-layout-width="62.9424" style="left:6pt;top:5.1248pt;line-height:11.52pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.6pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">1 × 150 = 150</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:327pt;top:33.424pt;width:164.2756pt;height:21.712pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.3472" data-layout-width="74.0784" style="left:6pt;top:5.1248pt;line-height:11.52pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.6pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">300 + 150 = 450</span></p>
</td>
</tr>

<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.5386" data-layout-width="223.7358" style="left:54pt;top:662.1599pt;line-height:14.76pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.3pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Stap 4 Verbind de cijfers aan het verhaal</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="41.2564" data-layout-width="474.8215" style="left:54pt;top:683.1326pt;line-height:13.1pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">TCK blijft € 300. TVK verdubbelt, maar TK stijgt van € 450 naar € 600 en verdubbelt niet. GCK halveert:</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">dezelfde € 300 wordt over tweemaal zoveel stickers verdeeld. GVK blijft € 1. Daardoor daalt GTK van € 3</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">naar € 2, niet naar € 1,50. Beide hoeveelheden passen binnen de capaciteit.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="38.1108" data-layout-width="466.8151" style="left:65pt;top:749.1222pt;line-height:12pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Onthoud</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51"> · Constant of variabel? Kijk naar het totale bedrag bij een andere Q.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Gemiddeld: deel het betreffende totaal door Q, met Q &gt; 0. Totaal is in € per periode; gemiddeld in € per product.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.4pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">GVK blijft alleen gelijk als de variabele kosten per product gelijk blijven. Hierna voeg je opbrengsten toe.</span></p>
</article>

<div class="page-break"></div>

## Startopgaven

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 1 · Even ophalen</b>

Een vereniging gebruikt de formule R = 40 + 3N voor het bedrag van een uitstap. R is in euro; N is het aantal deelnemers.

a) Bereken R bij N = 20.

b) Verdeel dat bedrag gelijk over de 20 deelnemers. Hoeveel betaalt ieder?
</div>

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 2 · Begripscheck</b>

Een drukker betaalt € 600 huur per maand en € 0,20 inkt per poster. Hij kan maximaal 5.000 posters per maand drukken.

a) Hij drukt deze maand 1.000 in plaats van 500 posters. Welke kostenpost verandert in totaal? Welke niet?

b) Een leerling noemt € 600 de GCK. Leg uit wat er aan die uitspraak niet klopt.
</div>

<div class="route"><b>Normale route:</b> Startopgaven → Begeleide inoefening → Zelfstandige oefening → Doeloefening.<br><b>Uitdagende route (minder tussenstappen, extra uitdaging):</b> Startopgaven → Zelfstandige oefening → Doeloefening → Denkertje / Bonusopgave.<br>Herhaling is extra bij beide routes.</div>

## Begeleide inoefening

*Begeleide inoefening hoort bij leren: je oefent met denkstappen en doet steeds meer zelf.*

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 3 · TasDruk — met denkstappen</b>

TasDruk betaalt € 120 per maand voor een werkruimte. Stof kost € 0,40 en inkt € 0,10 per tas. De capaciteit is 500 tassen per maand.

a) De werkruimte is een constante kostenpost. Maak de verklaring af: ‘Het totale maandbedrag blijft … als het aantal tassen …’

b) Vul aan: TCK = …; variabele kosten per tas = 0,40 + … = …; TVK = … × Q; TK = … + … × Q.

c) Neem de tabel over en vul de lege cellen in. Gebruik eerst TK = TCK + TVK; deel daarna door Q.

| Q (tassen per maand) | TCK (€ per maand) | TVK (€ per maand) | TK (€ per maand) | GTK (€ per tas) |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 200 | 120 | 100 | … | … |
| 400 | … | … | … | … |

d) Bereken ook GCK en GVK bij beide hoeveelheden. Maak af: ‘GTK daalt, omdat … over meer tassen worden verdeeld. GVK blijft …, omdat …’
</div>

<div class="page-break"></div>

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 4 · SleutelStudio — minder hulp</b>

SleutelStudio betaalt per maand € 90 huur en € 30 voor een vast abonnement. Materiaal kost € 1,20 en een verpakking € 0,30 per sleutelhanger. De capaciteit is 300 sleutelhangers per maand.

a) Deel de vier kostenposten in. Geef bij elke soort kosten één verklaring.

b) Stel TCK, TVK en TK op.

c) Bereken GCK, GVK en GTK bij Q = 100 en Q = 200. Leg uit welk gemiddelde halveert en welk niet.
</div>

## Zelfstandige oefening

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 5 · Twee veranderingen bij TafelTint</b>

TafelTint maakt placemats. In april is de maandhuur € 200 en kost materiaal € 1 per placemat. In mei is de maandhuur € 240 en kost materiaal € 0,80 per placemat. In beide maanden maakt het bedrijf 200 placemats. De capaciteit blijft 600 per maand. Er zijn geen andere kosten.

a) Bereken TCK, TVK en TK voor april en mei.

b) Bekijk de huurverhoging en de lagere materiaalprijs afzonderlijk. Welke verandering raakt de constante kosten? Welke de variabele kosten?

c) Een leerling zegt: ‘De huur stijgt, dus TK stijgt ook.’ Beoordeel dit met je uitkomsten.
</div>

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 6 · FietsGlans</b>

FietsGlans maakt fietsen schoon. Per maand betaalt het bedrijf € 240 huur en € 60 verzekering. Reinigingsmiddel kost € 0,60 en een beschermhoes € 0,40 per fiets. De capaciteit is 600 fietsen per maand.

a) Classificeer de kosten en stel TCK, TVK en TK op.

b) Bereken GCK, GVK en GTK bij 200 en 400 fietsen per maand. Noteer de eenheden.

c) Bereken ook TK bij beide hoeveelheden. Leg uit waarom TK stijgt, terwijl GTK daalt.
</div>

<div class="page-break"></div>

## Doeloefening

<div class="exercise target">
<b>Opgave 7 · Bakkerij De Korenaar</b>

De Korenaar kan maximaal **1.000 broden per maand** bakken. Deze maand blijven de ruimte en ovens hetzelfde. Tot en met die capaciteit gelden de volgende kosten:

| Kostenpost | Bedrag |
|---|---:|
| Huur en verzekering samen | € 440 per maand |
| Netaansluiting en energieabonnement samen | € 60 per maand |
| Meel en verpakking samen | € 0,70 per brood |
| Elektriciteitsverbruik van de ovens | € 0,10 per brood |

Q is het aantal broden per maand.

a) Neem de classificatietabel over en vul **alle lege cellen** in. Geef telkens aan hoe het totale bedrag reageert op een verandering van Q.

| Kostenpost | Constant of variabel? | Reden |
|---|---|---|
| Huur en verzekering | … | … |
| Netaansluiting en abonnement | … | … |
| Meel en verpakking | … | … |
| Elektriciteitsverbruik ovens | … | … |

b) Stel de functies voor TCK, TVK en TK op.

c) Bereken bij Q = 500 de GCK, GVK en GTK. Noteer de volledige eenheden.

d) Bereken bij Q = 1.000 de GCK, GVK en GTK. Noteer de volledige eenheden.

e) Vergelijk eerst TCK, TVK en TK bij beide hoeveelheden. Leg daarna met je uitkomsten uit waarom GCK halveert, GVK gelijk blijft en GTK niet halveert als Q verdubbelt. Je vergelijkt dezelfde maand en dezelfde productiecapaciteit.
</div>

<div class="box"><b>Kijk na en herstel</b><br>
Controleer niet alleen de getallen. Heb je bij de kostenindeling een reden gegeven? Staan de juiste eenheden bij de gemiddelden? Legt je laatste antwoord het verschil tussen totaal en gemiddeld uit?</div>

<div class="page-break"></div>

## Denkertje / Bonusopgave

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 8 · Eén rekening vertelt niet alles</b>

Een bedrijf maakt 100 stoffen vlaggetjes. De totale kosten zijn € 300. Over de kostenopbouw weet je verder niets.

a) Bedenk twee verschillende kostenfuncties die allebei bij deze gegevens passen. Gebruik positieve constante kosten en constante variabele kosten per vlaggetje.

b) Laat zien dat de twee functies bij 200 vlaggetjes verschillende totale kosten kunnen geven. Neem aan dat de capaciteit daarvoor groot genoeg is.

c) Beoordeel de uitspraak: ‘Als je TK bij één hoeveelheid kent, weet je ook hoeveel een verdubbeling van Q kost.’
</div>

## Herhaling en combineren

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 9 · Procenten</b> <span class="small">Herhaling §1.1.2</span>

Een sportvereniging heeft eerst 540 leden en later 648 leden. Bereken de procentuele stijging. Laat zien welk aantal je als uitgangspunt gebruikt.
</div>

<div class="exercise">
<b>Opgave 10 · Tabel lezen</b> <span class="small">Herhaling §1.1.3</span>

Een boottocht heeft de volgende prijstabel voor groepen.

| Aantal deelnemers | 5 | 10 | 15 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Totaal te betalen (€) | 20 | 30 | 40 |

a) Hoeveel betaalt een groep van 10 deelnemers samen? Hoeveel is dat per persoon?

b) Met hoeveel euro stijgt het totaalbedrag als er 5 deelnemers bijkomen? Hoeveel is dat per extra deelnemer?
</div>

<div class="box"><b>Terug naar de openingsvraag</b><br>
Bij Fles & Co verdubbelt het totale materiaalbedrag als de productie verdubbelt. De huur blijft gelijk. Daarom verdubbelen de totale kosten niet. Het bedrijf maakt meer flessen, maar verdeelt dezelfde huur over meer producten.</div>

<div class="page-break"></div>

<h1 class="native-text" data-layout-height="24.6" data-layout-width="340.0322" style="left:54pt;top:48.5501pt;line-height:21.6pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:18pt;font-weight:800;color:#18364b">2.1.2 · Opbrengsten: totaal en per product</span></h1>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="67.1787" style="left:54pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Opbrengsten · 10</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="40.7345" style="left:145.3319pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Winst · 11</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="60.2184" style="left:210.211pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Break-even · 11</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="45.5829" style="left:294.5717pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Grafiek · 12</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="490.4487" style="left:54pt;top:112.2443pt;line-height:14.19pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Een volle kassa: is dat winst?</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> Een wafelverkoper ontvangt € 500 op één dag. Maar de ingrediënten en</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">de kraam moeten ook worden betaald. De kassa toont de opbrengst; wat blijft er na alle kosten over?</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="226.4662" style="left:66.2pt;top:155.6243pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Lesdoelen · opbrengsten, winst en break-even</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="40.7997" data-layout-width="452.0892" style="left:66.2pt;top:172.7694pt;line-height:12.9pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Je kunt TO, GO en winst berekenen en uitleggen waarom bij een vaste prijs GO = P. Je kunt break-even</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">algebraïsch bepalen en vertalen naar hele producten. Je kunt TK en TO tekenen en winst of verlies als</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">verticale afstand aangeven.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="18.5997" data-layout-width="282.9238" style="left:54pt;top:229.8239pt;line-height:15.5997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Opbrengst is het bedrag dat de verkoop oplevert</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="489.0437" style="left:54pt;top:250.7243pt;line-height:14.19pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">P</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> is de verkoopprijs per product. </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Q</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> is de afzet: het aantal verkochte producten in de gekozen periode.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Hieronder verkopen we </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">100 wafels per dag voor € 5 per stuk</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="236.172" style="left:66.2pt;top:295.1489pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Totale opbrengst (TO) · alle verkopen samen</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="461.0755" style="left:66.2pt;top:313.5843pt;line-height:14.19pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">De totale opbrengst is het volledige verkoopbedrag. Een ander woord is </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">omzet</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. Er zijn nog geen</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">kosten afgetrokken.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="21" data-layout-width="76.74" style="left:66.2pt;top:349.2192pt;line-height:18pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:15pt;font-weight:700;color:#176d92">TO = P × Q</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="29.145" data-layout-width="92.859" style="left:300.9024pt;top:353.4675pt;line-height:13.545pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">TO = 5 × 100</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO = € 500 per dag</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.7598" data-layout-width="82.8586" style="left:66.2pt;top:372.4101pt;line-height:11.7598pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.7998pt;font-weight:400;color:#3f5d71">Hier: euro per dag.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.4" data-layout-width="272.844" style="left:66.2pt;top:410.1509pt;line-height:14.4pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Gemiddelde opbrengst (GO) · per verkocht product</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="303.7266" style="left:66.2pt;top:428.5863pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Deel de totale opbrengst door het aantal verkochte producten.</span></p>
<span aria-label="TO gedeeld door Q" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:107.2pt;top:448.0828pt;width:25.5146pt;height:36.1428pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="19.9198" data-layout-width="22.5283" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:16.9198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:14.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#187e74">TO</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="19.9198" data-layout-width="14.3927" style="left:7.0609pt;top:19.223pt;line-height:16.9198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:14.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#187e74">Q</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:17.7212pt;border-top:0.7pt solid #187e74"></span></span>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="21" data-layout-width="42" style="left:66.2pt;top:455.1965pt;line-height:18pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:15pt;font-weight:700;color:#187e74">GO = </span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.1595" data-layout-width="30.7685" style="left:138.3599pt;top:460.8228pt;line-height:11.1595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.2996pt;font-weight:400;color:#187e74">(Q &gt; 0)</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="29.145" data-layout-width="90.6435" style="left:300.9024pt;top:461.4025pt;line-height:13.545pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">GO = 500 / 100</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">GO = € 5 per wafel</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.7598" data-layout-width="90.4241" style="left:66.2pt;top:487.4681pt;line-height:11.7598pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.7998pt;font-weight:400;color:#3f5d71">Hier: euro per wafel.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="477.4858" style="left:54pt;top:516.1644pt;line-height:14.1899pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Bij een vaste prijs geldt GO = P:</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> iedere verkochte wafel levert € 5 op. Meer verkopen verhoogt dan</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">TO, maar niet GO. Bij Q = 0 kun je geen gemiddelde berekenen: delen door nul kan niet.</span></p>
<figure class="native-figure" style="left:54pt;top:550pt;width:491.2756pt;height:67.5267pt"><img alt="Figuur 1. Opbrengst is nog geen winst: trek eerst alle kosten af." src="_assets/theory-20260921/page-012.svg"/><figcaption class="native-text" data-layout-height="13.9195" data-layout-width="253.5942" style="left:0pt;top:70.5267pt;line-height:10.9195pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.0996pt;font-weight:400;color:#506979">Figuur 1. Opbrengst is nog geen winst: trek eerst alle kosten af.</span></figcaption></figure>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="207.239" style="left:64pt;top:650.1694pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Ons voorbeeld op p. 11–12 · WafelWagen</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.3592" data-layout-width="126.5088" style="left:64pt;top:668.3279pt;line-height:12.3592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Verkoopprijs: </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">€ 5 per wafel</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.3592" data-layout-width="192.3967" style="left:306.6378pt;top:668.3279pt;line-height:12.3592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Ingrediënten en verpakking: </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">€ 2 per wafel</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.3592" data-layout-width="102.7285" style="left:64pt;top:684.6149pt;line-height:12.3592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Kraam: </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">€ 250 per dag</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.3592" data-layout-width="142.3642" style="left:306.6378pt;top:684.6149pt;line-height:12.3592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Capaciteit: </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">150 wafels per dag</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.2993pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="218.5803" style="left:64pt;top:711.5333pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO = 5Q    en    TK = 250 + 2Q   </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">(euro per dag)</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="27.9996" data-layout-width="474.3703" style="left:54pt;top:740.7284pt;line-height:12.9999pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Onze afspraak:</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> alle gemaakte producten worden verkocht, tenzij anders vermeld. Daarom is Q hier zowel de</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">productie als de afzet. Vergelijk steeds bedragen uit </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">dezelfde periode</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
</article>

<h1 class="native-text" data-layout-height="24.6" data-layout-width="223.986" style="left:54pt;top:48.5501pt;line-height:21.6pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:18pt;font-weight:800;color:#18364b">2.1.2 · Winst en break-even</span></h1>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="66.547" style="left:54pt;top:86.0238pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Opbrengsten · 10</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="41.0828" style="left:144.6991pt;top:86.0238pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Winst · 11</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="60.9798" style="left:209.9217pt;top:86.0238pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Break-even · 11</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="45.5829" style="left:295.0214pt;top:86.0238pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Grafiek · 12</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="22.1997" data-layout-width="212.7238" style="left:54pt;top:109.8681pt;line-height:19.1997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:15.9998pt;font-weight:800;color:#18364b">Winst </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#4b6574;margin-left:7.9754pt">Wat blijft er na alle kosten over?</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="27.6432" data-layout-width="216.1268" style="left:192.4402pt;top:140.4705pt;line-height:12.524pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Trek de totale kosten af van de totale opbrengst.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Een </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">negatieve winst</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> noemen we </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">verlies</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="21" data-layout-width="114.255" style="left:66.2pt;top:143.7922pt;line-height:18pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:15pt;font-weight:700;color:#187e74">Winst = TO − TK</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.7195" data-layout-width="375.6936" style="left:54pt;top:178.7401pt;line-height:12.7195pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">WafelWagen: </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO = 5Q</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> en </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TK = 250 + 2Q</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. Alle totale bedragen zijn euro per dag.</span></p>

<td style="left:0pt;top:75.68pt;width:58.9531pt;height:20.62pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1192" data-layout-width="20.5729" style="left:6pt;top:4.2485pt;line-height:12.1192pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">100</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:58.9531pt;top:75.68pt;width:122.8189pt;height:20.62pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1192" data-layout-width="66.0604" style="left:53.7559pt;top:4.2485pt;line-height:12.1192pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">5 × 100 = 500</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:181.772pt;top:75.68pt;width:171.9465pt;height:20.62pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1192" data-layout-width="94.6618" style="left:74.281pt;top:4.2485pt;line-height:12.1192pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">250 + 2 × 100 = 450</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:353.7184pt;top:75.68pt;width:137.5571pt;height:20.62pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.1192" data-layout-width="71.393" style="left:63.1606pt;top:4.2485pt;line-height:12.1192pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.0994pt;font-weight:700;color:#187469">500 − 450 = 50</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="29.2875" data-layout-width="490.6542" style="left:54pt;top:301.8521pt;line-height:13.568pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Bij 50 wafels is er </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">€ 100 verlies</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">; bij 100 wafels </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">€ 50 winst</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. Trek óók de € 250 voor de kraam af, niet alleen</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">de ingrediënten en verpakking. Zonder verkoop is er nog steeds € 250 aan kosten.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="22.1997" data-layout-width="213.4174" style="left:54pt;top:348.9641pt;line-height:19.1997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:15.9998pt;font-weight:800;color:#18364b">Break-even </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#4b6574;margin-left:7.9599pt">Geen winst, geen verlies</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="29.2875" data-layout-width="459.7511" style="left:54pt;top:373.7881pt;line-height:13.568pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">De opbrengst dekt </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">precies alle kosten</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">; de winst is nul. Dat betekent niet dat TO en TK nul zijn. Het</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">bijbehorende verkochte aantal heet de </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">break-even-afzet</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.5198" data-layout-width="121.8846" style="left:66.2pt;top:415.8842pt;line-height:11.5198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5999pt;font-weight:400;color:#435f70">ALLE PRODUCTEN SAMEN</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.5198" data-layout-width="114.9054" style="left:315.3378pt;top:415.8842pt;line-height:11.5198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5999pt;font-weight:400;color:#435f70">PER PRODUCT · BIJ Q &gt; 0</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="22.1997" data-layout-width="62.3111" style="left:66.2pt;top:430.5882pt;line-height:19.1997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:15.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO = TK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="22.1997" data-layout-width="76.7749" style="left:315.3378pt;top:430.5882pt;line-height:19.1997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:15.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">GO = GTK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.7598" data-layout-width="143.8722" style="left:66.2pt;top:452.9801pt;line-height:11.7598pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.7998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Totale opbrengst = totale kosten.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.7598" data-layout-width="221.9864" style="left:315.3378pt;top:452.9801pt;line-height:11.7598pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.7998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Gemiddelde opbrengst = gemiddelde totale kosten.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="27.7998" data-layout-width="477.7607" style="left:54pt;top:479.5323pt;line-height:12.8pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Waarom ook per product?</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> Deel beide totalen door dezelfde Q: TO / Q = TK / Q, dus GO = GTK. Omdat hier </span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">GO = P, geldt bij break-even ook </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">P = GTK</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.7997" data-layout-width="220.4949" style="left:54pt;top:512.7323pt;line-height:13.7997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.4998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Berekenen · stel TO en TK aan elkaar gelijk</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="59.7595" data-layout-width="114.2821" style="left:64pt;top:538.1923pt;line-height:14.52pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">5Q = 250 + 2Q</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">5Q − 2Q = 250</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">3Q = 250</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Q = 250 / 3 = 83,333…</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="58.4997" data-layout-width="180.9558" style="left:305.8924pt;top:538.7823pt;line-height:14.5pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Vul de opbrengsten- en kostenfunctie in.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Trek aan beide kanten 2Q af.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Voeg de Q-termen samen.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Deel door 3: de break-even-afzet.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="29.2875" data-layout-width="459.9532" style="left:54pt;top:610.0361pt;line-height:13.568pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">De lijnen snijden elkaar bij </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Q ≈ 83,33 wafels per dag</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. Reken verder met de ongeronde Q. Een echte</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">verkoper verkoopt alleen </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">hele wafels</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.5996pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.7997" data-layout-width="343.1053" style="left:54pt;top:644.7483pt;line-height:13.7997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.4998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Hele producten · controleer het aantal onder én boven het snijpunt</span></p>

<h1 class="native-text" data-layout-height="24.6" data-layout-width="337.782" style="left:54pt;top:48.5501pt;line-height:21.6pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:18pt;font-weight:800;color:#18364b">2.1.2 · Opbrengsten en winst in de grafiek</span></h1>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="66.547" style="left:54pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Opbrengsten · 10</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="40.7345" style="left:144.6991pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Winst · 11</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="60.2184" style="left:209.5782pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:400;color:#597080">Break-even · 11</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="12.7198" data-layout-width="46.0689" style="left:293.9389pt;top:86.0643pt;line-height:9.7198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:8.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Grafiek · 12</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="18.5997" data-layout-width="220.0959" style="left:54pt;top:111.5539pt;line-height:15.5997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Teken eerst de assen, daarna de lijnen</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="477.1399" style="left:54pt;top:132.4543pt;line-height:14.19pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Voor WafelWagen staat </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Q (wafels per dag)</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> op de horizontale as. Op de verticale as staan </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO en TK</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">(euro per dag)</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. Kies een schaal tot 150 wafels en € 800.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="389.1806" style="left:54pt;top:166.8343pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Voor elke rechte lijn zijn twee punten genoeg. Een punt noteer je als </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">(Q; bedrag)</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>

<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="471.7852" style="left:54pt;top:269.5243pt;line-height:14.19pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Verbind de punten van dezelfde lijn, geef de lijn een naam en teken haar tot de </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">capaciteit van 150</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">wafels</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. TO begint bij nul; TK begint bij € 250.</span></p>
<figure class="native-figure" style="left:54pt;top:306pt;width:491.2756pt;height:217.7979pt"><img alt="Figuur 2. Lees TO en TK af bij dezelfde Q. Het verschil is de winst of het verlies per dag." src="_assets/theory-20260921/page-014.svg"/><figcaption class="native-text" data-layout-height="13.9195" data-layout-width="349.7861" style="left:0pt;top:220.7979pt;line-height:10.9195pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.0996pt;font-weight:400;color:#506979">Figuur 2. Lees TO en TK af bij dezelfde Q. Het verschil is de winst of het verlies per dag.</span></figcaption></figure>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="35.9765" style="left:62pt;top:557.2206pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Verlies</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="58.7676" style="left:228.0919pt;top:557.2206pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Break-even</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="32.4347" style="left:394.1837pt;top:557.2206pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Winst</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="19.7994" data-layout-width="54.8822" style="left:62pt;top:574.0458pt;line-height:16.7994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO &lt; TK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="19.7994" data-layout-width="54.8822" style="left:228.0919pt;top:574.0458pt;line-height:16.7994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO = TK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="19.7994" data-layout-width="54.8821" style="left:394.1837pt;top:574.0458pt;line-height:16.7994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">TO &gt; TK</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="27.4997" data-layout-width="121.5271" style="left:62pt;top:595.4456pt;line-height:12.5pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">TO ligt onder TK.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Hier: links van het snijpunt.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="27.4997" data-layout-width="106.5374" style="left:228.0919pt;top:595.4456pt;line-height:12.5pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">De lijnen snijden elkaar.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">De winst is nul.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="27.4997" data-layout-width="128.6972" style="left:394.1837pt;top:595.4456pt;line-height:12.5pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">TO ligt boven TK.</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Hier: rechts van het snijpunt.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="18.5997" data-layout-width="285.7447" style="left:54pt;top:637.0002pt;line-height:15.5997pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Winst en verlies aflezen · kijk bij één hoeveelheid</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="485.3208" style="left:54pt;top:657.9006pt;line-height:14.19pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Bij </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Q = 100</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> lees je TO = € 500 en TK = € 450 af. Het verschil is </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">€ 50 winst per dag</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. Bij </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Q = 50</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> zijn de</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">opbrengsten € 250 en de kosten € 350: </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">€ 100 verlies per dag</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.1994" data-layout-width="195.1725" style="left:66.2pt;top:702.2806pt;line-height:13.1994pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Een verticale afstand, géén oppervlakte</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="30.3894" data-layout-width="458.4414" style="left:66.2pt;top:719.4706pt;line-height:14.19pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">Deze verticale as toont </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">totale bedragen</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">. Trek bij één Q de twee hoogten van elkaar af. Bereken</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">hier </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">geen driehoek of rechthoek</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.9995pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">: een oppervlakte tussen TO en TK is geen winstbedrag.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="27.6997" data-layout-width="470.0486" style="left:54pt;top:763.7056pt;line-height:12.7pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">Het model is doorlopend; echte wafels zijn heel.</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> Q ≈ 83,33 is het snijpunt. Het eerste gehele aantal zonder</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b">verlies is </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:700;color:#18364b">84 wafels</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.9998pt;font-weight:400;color:#18364b"> (controle op p. 11).</span></p>
</article>
